# Agents · S · part 4

996 entries, scaffold-entity to schema-markup. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **scaffold-entity**: Scaffolds a new entity type (e.g. Quotes, Locations, Factions) into FanHub with a full-stack implementation: backend model, DbContext registration, seed data, EF Core migration, REST controller, frontend model, homepage stat card + nav-card, header navigation link, and a Blazor list page with an inline add form. After scaffolding, verifies seed data accuracy against canonical Breaking Bad sources. Trigger phrases: "add a [Entity] entity", "scaffold [Entity]", "add [Entity] to FanHub", "create a [Entity] page", "add [Entity] with seed data". ([MSBart2/CopilotTraining](https://github.com/MSBart2/CopilotTraining/blob/HEAD/tech-talks/copilot-plugins/examples/fanhub-plugin/.github/agents/scaffold-entity.agent.md) · ★2)
- **scaffold-externals**: Integration analyst — scans features for external dependencies and classifies them. Read-only. ([magpiexyz-lab/TipGuard](https://github.com/magpiexyz-lab/TipGuard/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/scaffold-externals.md))
- **scaffold-factory-agent**: **Critical Distinction:** - You do NOT produce scaffolds for specific topics - You DO produce scaffold TEMPLATES that work across entire CATEGORIES of topics - Your outputs are MODULAR PROMPT COMPONENTS with {{variable}} placeholders - Your scaffolds are designed to be COMPOSED, COMBINED, and REUSED ([pur3v4d3r/pur3-pkb-codebase](https://github.com/pur3v4d3r/pur3-pkb-codebase/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/agents/scaffold-factory-agent.md) · ★6 · 2 copies)
- **scaffold-feature**: Use when asked to add a new feature that needs a new DB table, a new DTO, and/or a new route in this SvelteKit app (Homerun) — e.g. "add a new X entity", "add a page for managing Y", "add a DTO for Z". Scaffolds schema.ts changes, the DTO class extending BaseDTO, and route files, following this repo's exact conventions (no manual route-file typing, no raw Drizzle in routes, toJSON() before returning from load, shared-resource finders). Not for one-off bugfixes or edits to existing routes — use the general agent for those. ([zedensix/homerun](https://github.com/zedensix/homerun/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/scaffold-feature.md) · ★4 · 2 copies)
- **scaffold-feature**: Use when asked to add a feature that needs a new DB table, a new DTO, a new route or a new background job in localassistant, e.g. "add rooms", "add a page for scenes", "add a DTO for camera streams". Scaffolds the schema change and migration, the DTO class extending BaseDTO, the route files under (protected)/, the zod schema and, for slow work, the job and its worker handler, following this repo's exact conventions. Not for one-off bug fixes or edits to existing routes. ([zedensix/localassistant](https://github.com/zedensix/localassistant/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/scaffold-feature.md))
- **scaffold-feature**: Use when asked to add a feature that needs a new DB table, a new DTO, a new route or a new background job in opencount, e.g. "add recurring expenses", "add a page for managing group members", "add a DTO for settlements". Scaffolds the schema change and migration, the DTO class extending BaseDTO, the route files under (protected)/, the zod schema and, for slow work, the job and its worker handler, following this repo's exact conventions. Not for one-off bug fixes or edits to existing routes. ([zedensix/opencount](https://github.com/zedensix/opencount/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/scaffold-feature.md))
- **scaffold-front**: Frontend infrastructure scaffolding specialist. Use before the first frontend tests or when the frontend project structure does not yet exist. Produces project setup, component scaffolding, service layer stubs, routing, and test harness (bUnit + Playwright). Never writes business logic — only plumbing and structure. ([FoxSilou/ConfigurationClaude](https://github.com/FoxSilou/ConfigurationClaude/blob/HEAD/frontend/.claude/agents/scaffold-front.md))
- **scaffold-generator**: Project scaffold generation agent — initializes a working project from architecture specifications. Creates directory structure, package configs, Docker setup, CI/CD pipelines, environment templates, and CLAUDE.md. Output builds and lints clean on first run. ([ShaheerKhawaja/ProductionOS](https://github.com/ShaheerKhawaja/ProductionOS/blob/HEAD/agents/scaffold-generator.md) · ★8)
- **scaffold-generator**: Generates a single product-code scaffold by reading the matching template, substituting manifest-driven values, and writing the file to the user's repo. Idempotent. Invoke from /sbd-deploy or /sbd-add. ([ankush1982/ai-secure-by-design](https://github.com/ankush1982/ai-secure-by-design/blob/HEAD/agents/scaffold-generator.md) · ★4)
- **scaffold-guardian**: Supervisiona a geração do scaffold do projeto e garante conformidade com padrões de segurança. ([lucastigrereal-dev/automation-factory-os](https://github.com/lucastigrereal-dev/automation-factory-os/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/scaffold-guardian.md))
- **scaffold-harmonizer**: Runs once per scaffolding pass, after every sprint is filled. Runs the deviation gate over the pass's Exec-{Abbrev}/ tree, restores the template wording for every Class A and Class B deviation, and re-runs the gate until it reports zero. Reports Class C and every other finding to the caller and edits nothing beyond headings and formatting lines. ([gabgoss/planwise](https://github.com/gabgoss/planwise/blob/HEAD/plugins/planwise/agents/scaffold-harmonizer.md) · ★3)

- **scaffold-inspector**: Inspect the mvp/ folder and return a structured report on what's already scaffolded — framework, router, state library, design tokens, motion library, conventions in use, gaps. - **scaffold-integrity-auditor**: Audits one attempt's diff for content that recognises evaluation cases instead of solving them and for measurement tampering in an agent scaffold: evaluation identifiers, memorised answers or task-specific branches whose source is an answer key, a grading target or a development outcome; reads of protected paths; edits to the evaluator or its settings. Distinguishes the runner's public interface from answer-bearing special cases by provenance. Reports pass, fail or inconclusive with file and line.
